# Refactor renderer for multi-group split view support

## User

Refactor the rendering in ~/code/rust-editor/src/main.rs to support multiple editor groups with split views.

## Current State
- Single-group rendering in `render_impl()` and `render_impl_with_perf()` (debug version)
- Uses `model.editor()` and `model.document()` for the focused editor/document
- Renders to full window

## Required Changes

### 1. Add constants for tab bar height
At the top of main.rs, add:
```rust
const TAB_BAR_HEIGHT: usize = 28;
```

### 2. Create helper methods in Renderer

Add these methods to the Renderer impl:

#### `render_all_groups()` - Main entry point for multi-group rendering
- Call `model.editor_area.compute_layout()` with window rect (accounting for status bar)
- Iterate over all groups, render each in its computed rect
- Render splitter bars between groups

#### `render_editor_group()` - Render a single group
Parameters: buffer, model, group_id, group_rect, is_focused
- Render tab bar at top of rect
- Render editor content in remaining space (after tab bar)
- If is_focused, draw focus indicator (border or highlight)

#### `render_tab_bar()` - Render tabs for a group
Parameters: buffer, model, group, rect
- Draw background
- Draw each tab with filename, modified indicator
- Active tab has different background color

#### `render_splitters()` - Render splitter bars
Parameters: buffer, splitters, theme
- Draw each splitter bar using theme.splitter colors

### 3. Modify render_impl() and render_impl_with_perf()

Replace the single-group rendering with:
1. Clear screen with editor background
2. Compute layout
3. Render all groups via `render_all_groups()`
4. Render splitters
5. Render status bar (unchanged, at bottom)
6. Present buffer

### 4. Key considerations

- Each group has its own viewport, cursor, selections
- When rendering a group, use that group's editor via `model.editor_area.editors.get(&editor_id)`
- Clip rendering to group rect (don't draw outside bounds)
- Tab bar filename comes from `document.file_path`
- Focus indicator: draw 2px border on focused group in accent color

### 5. Implementation approach

For simplicity, modify `render_impl()` first:
1. After clearing, call `model.editor_area.compute_layout(available_rect)`
2. For each group_id in `model.editor_area.groups.keys()`:
   - Get the group's rect from `group.rect`
   - Get the active editor_id from `group.active_editor_id()`
   - Get the editor and document
   - Render content clipped to group rect
3. Render splitters
4. Status bar last

Start with the simpler non-perf version. The perf version can duplicate the logic.

Use the existing draw_text function for text rendering.

After making changes, verify with: cargo build 2>&1 | head -30

Return a summary of the changes made.

// Identifiers\n13: // ============================================================================\n14: \n15: /// Unique identifier for a document\n16: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]\n17: pub struct DocumentId(pub u64);\n18: \n19: /// Unique identifier for an editor view\n20: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]\n21: pub struct EditorId(pub u64);\n22: \n23: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]\n29: pub struct TabId(pub u64);\n30: \n31: // ============================================================================\n32: // Layout Primitives\n33: // ============================================================================\n34: \n35: /// Rectangle for layout calculations\n36: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Default)]\n37: pub struct Rect {\n38:     pub x: f32,\n39:     pub y: f32,\n40:     pub width: f32,\n41:     pub height: f32,\n42: }\n43: \n44: impl Rect {\n45:     pub fn new(x: f32, y: f32, width: f32, height: f32) -> Self {\n46:         Self {\n47:             x,\n48:             y,\n49:             width,\n50:             height,\n51:         }\n52:     }\n53: \n54:     pub fn contains(&self, px: f32, py: f32) -> bool {\n55:         px >= self.x && px < self.x + self.width && py >= self.y && py < self.y + self.height\n56:     }\n57: }\n58: \n59: // ============================================================================\n60: // Tabs\n61: // ============================================================================\n62: \n63: /// A tab represents an open editor in a group\n64: #[derive(Debug, Clone)]\n65: pub struct Tab {\n66:     pub id: TabId,\n67:     pub editor_id: EditorId,\n68:     pub is_pinned: bool,\n69:     pub is_preview: bool, // Preview tabs get replaced on next file open\n70: }\n71: \n72: // ============================================================================\n73: // Editor Group (pane with tabs)\n74: // ============================================================================\n75: \n76: /// An editor group contains a tab bar and displays one editor at a time\n77: #[derive(Debug, Clone)]\n78: pub struct EditorGroup {\n79:     pub id: GroupId,\n80:     pub tabs: Vec<Tab>,\n81:     pub active_tab_index: usize,\n82:     /// Layout info (set by parent during layout computation)\n83:     pub rect: Rect,\n84: }\n85: \n86: impl EditorGroup {\n87:     /// Get the currently active tab\n88:     pub fn active_tab(&self) -> Option<&Tab> {\n89:         self.tabs.get(self.active_tab_index)\n90:     }\n91: \n92:     /// Get the editor ID of the active tab\n93:     pub fn active_editor_id(&self) -> Option<EditorId> {\n94:         self.active_tab().map(|t| t.editor_id)\n95:     }\n96: }\n97: \n98: // ============================================================================\n99: // Layout Tree\n100: // ============================================================================\n101: \n102: /// Direction for splitting editor groups\n103: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]\n104: pub enum SplitDirection {\n105:     /// Children arranged left-to-right\n106:     Horizontal,\n107:     /// Children arranged top-to-bottom\n108:     Vertical,\n109: }\n110: \n111: /// A container that splits space between children\n112: #[derive(Debug, Clone)]\n113: pub struct SplitContainer {\n114:     pub direction: SplitDirection,\n115:     pub children: Vec<LayoutNode>,\n116:     /// Proportional sizes (0.0 to 1.0, must sum to 1.0)\n117:     pub ratios: Vec<f32>,\n118:     /// Minimum size in pixels for each child\n119:     pub min_sizes: Vec<f32>,\n120: }\n121: \n122: /// A node in the layout tree - either a group or a split container\n123: #[derive(Debug, Clone)]\n124: pub enum LayoutNode {\n125:     Group(GroupId),\n126:     Split(SplitContainer),\n127: }\n128: \n129: // ============================================================================\n130: // Editor Area (top-level container)\n131: // ============================================================================\n132: \n133: /// The editor area manages all groups, documents, and the layout tree\n134: #[derive(Debug, Clone)]\n135: pub struct EditorArea {\n136:     /// All open documents (shared across editors)\n137:     pub documents: HashMap<DocumentId, Document>,\n138: \n139:     /// All editor states (each references a document)\n140:     pub editors: HashMap<EditorId, EditorState>,\n141: \n142:     /// All editor groups (each contains tabs)\n143:     pub groups: HashMap<GroupId, EditorGroup>,\n144: \n145:     /// The layout tree root\n146:     pub layout: LayoutNode,\n147: \n148:     /// Currently focused group\n149:     pub focused_group_id: GroupId,\n150: \n151:     /// ID generators\n152:     next_document_id: u64,\n153:     next_editor_id: u64,\n154:     next_group_id: u64,\n155:     next_tab_id: u64,\n156: }\n157: \n158: impl EditorArea {\n159:     /// Create a new editor area with a single document and editor.\n160:     /// This is the migration path from the old single-pane architecture.\n161:     pub fn single_document(mut document: Document, mut editor: let mut documents = HashMap::new();\n173:         let mut editors = HashMap::new();\n174:         let mut groups = HashMap::new();\n175: \n176:         documents.insert(doc_id, document);\n177: {\n            self.width = model.window_size.0;\n            self.height = model.window_size.1;\n            self.surface\n                .resize(\n                    NonZeroU32::new(self.width).unwrap(),\n                    NonZeroU32::new(self.height).unwrap(),\n                )\n                .map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(\"Failed to resize surface: {}\", e))?;\n        }\n\n        let mut buffer = self\n            .surface\n            .buffer_mut()\n            .map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(\"Failed to get surface buffer: {}\", e))?;\n\n        let bg_color = model.theme.editor.background.to_argb_u32();\n        buffer.fill(bg_color);\n\n        let line_height = self.line_height();\n        let font_size = self.font_size;\n        let ascent = self.line_metrics.ascent;\n        let char_width = self.char_width;\n        let width = self.width;\n        let height = self.height;\n\n        let status_bar_height = line_height;\n        let available_rect = Rect::new(\n            0.0,\n            0.0,\n            width as f32,\n            (height as usize).saturating_sub(status_bar_height) as f32,\n        );\n        let splitters = model.editor_area.compute_layout(available_rect);\n\n        self.render_all_groups(&mut buffer, model, &splitters);\n\n        let status_bar_bg = model.theme.status_bar.background.to_argb_u32();\n        let status_bar_fg = model.theme.status_bar.foreground.to_argb_u32();\n        let status_y = (height as usize).saturating_sub(status_bar_height);\n        for py in status_y..height as usize {\n            for px in 0..width as usize {\n                buffer[py * width as usize + px] = status_bar_bg;\n            }\n        }\n\n        let available_chars = (width as f32 / char_width).floor() as usize;\n        let layout = model.ui.status_bar.layout(available_chars);\n\n        for seg in &layout.left {\n            let x_px = (seg.x as f32 * char_width).round() as usize;\n            draw_text(\n                &mut buffer,\n                &self.font,\n                &mut self.glyph_cache,\n                font_size,\n                ascent,\n                width,\n                height,\n                x_px,\n                status_y + 2,\n                &seg.text,\n                status_bar_fg,\n            );\n        }\n\n        for seg in self.glyph_cache,\n                font_size,\n                ascent,\n                width,\n                height,\n                x_px,\n                status_y + 2,\n All 293 tests pass. Let me provide a summary of the changes made:

## Summary of Changes

Refactored rendering in [~/code/rust-editor/src/main.rs](file://~/code/rust-editor/src/main.rs) to support multiple editor groups with split views:

### 1. Added imports and constant
- Added imports for `GroupId`, `Rect`, and `SplitterBar` from `editor_area` module
- Added `TAB_BAR_HEIGHT: usize = 28` constant

### 2. New static rendering methods added to `Renderer` impl:

- **`render_all_groups_static()`** - Main entry point for multi-group rendering. Computes layout, iterates over all groups, and renders each group and splitters.

- **`render_editor_group_static()`** - Renders a single editor group including:
  - Tab bar at top
  - Current line highlight
  - Selection highlight
  - Line numbers and text content
  - Cursors
  - Gutter border
  - Focus indicator (2px border when focused, only when multiple groups exist)

- **`render_tab_bar_static()`** - Renders the tab bar with:
  - Background and border
  - Active/inactive tab styling
  - Modified indicator (● prefix)
  - Filename display

- **`render_splitters_static()`** - Renders splitter bars between groups using theme colors

### 3. Updated `render_impl()` and `render_impl_with_perf()`:
- Changed `model` parameter from `&AppModel` to `&mut AppModel` (needed for `compute_layout()`)
- Compute layout for available area (window minus status bar)
- Render all groups via the new static methods
- Status bar rendering unchanged

### 4. Key architecture decisions:
- Used static methods to avoid borrow checker issues with `self.surface.buffer_mut()` and calling other methods
- Each group renders independently within its computed rect
- Tab bar height subtracted from editor content area
- All rendering is clipped to group bounds
